import { identityRequest } from "./identity-request.ts";
import { randomUUID, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdirSync, openSync, closeSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, renameSync, unlinkSync, lstatSync, fsyncSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import type { ServeHost } from "./proof.ts";

export interface StagingAccount {
  kind: "user"; role: string; email: string; password: string; principal: string; token: string; expiresAt: number;
}
export type StagingSessions = Record<string, StagingAccount[]>;
export type ProvisionedAccount = StagingAccount;
export type ProvisionedSessions = StagingSessions;
export interface ProvisionAccountsOptions extends SeedAccountsOptions {
  /** Named persistent roster within this deployment. Default default. */
  roster?: string;
  /** Required for a host not returned by startServe. Keep with the deployment. */
  rosterDir?: string;
}
export interface SeedAccountsOptions {
  roles: Record<string, number>;
  provision: { readonly name: string };
  /** Service seat authorized by the provisioning mutation. Default operator. */
  service?: string;
  args?: (account: StagingAccount) => Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Whole setup deadline, including rate-limit waits. Default 5 minutes. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

function validatePlan(options: SeedAccountsOptions) {
  // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — custom argument keys override defaults; the seat grants no extra authority.
  const service = options.service ?? "operator";
  const roles = Object.entries(options.roles), timeout = options.timeoutMs ?? 300_000;
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !roles.length || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)
      || roles.reduce((sum, [, n]) => sum + n, 0) > 100 || !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/.test(options.provision?.name ?? "")) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: provide 1..100 accounts across named roles, a mutation handle and a positive timeoutMs");
  }
  return {service, roles, timeout};
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
/** @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_STAGING for production/dev hosts; E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup. Partial accounts/grants may exist; inspect or discard the staging store before retrying. */
async function accounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions, environment: "staging" | "production" | "development", roster?: Roster, save: () => void = () => {}): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const {service, roles, timeout} = validatePlan(options);
  const end = performance.now() + timeout;
  const partial = environment === "staging" ? "inspect or discard this staging deployment" : "inspect partial production accounts/grants before retrying";
  const remaining = () => {
    const ms = Math.ceil(end - performance.now());
    if (ms <= 0) throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: setup incomplete; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
    return ms;
  };
  const request = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) => fetch(host.url + path, { ...init, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(Math.min(remaining(), 30_000)) });
  const config = await request("/auth/config");
  const identity = await config.json();
  if (!config.ok || identity.environment !== environment || identity.development !== (environment === "development") || !identity.password) {
    throw Error(environment === "development" ? "E_DEV_ACCOUNTS: devAccounts requires startDev with password identity; serve hosts refuse before creating users" : environment === "staging"
      ? "E_STAGING: account seeding requires serve on a fresh deploy --staging with password identity; production and dev refuse before creating users"
      : "E_PRODUCTION_ACCOUNTS: account provisioning requires serve on a production deploy with password identity; staging and dev refuse before creating users");
  }
  const operator = await host.operatorToken(service);
  if (environment === "production") {
    const response = await request("/schema", { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` } }), schema = await response.json();
    const named = response.ok && Array.isArray(schema.service_identities) && schema.service_identities.includes(`service:${service}`);
    if (!named) process.stderr.write(`snapback4: WARNING minted service:${service}, but the served schema reports no explicit positive authorization mention for that service identity; rules still decide every operation and provisionAccounts(${options.provision.name}) will likely refuse.\n`);
  }
  const sessions: StagingSessions = Object.create(null);
  for (const [role, count] of roles) {
    sessions[role] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      let fresh = false;
      let entry = roster?.entries.find(entry => entry.role === role && entry.index === i);
      if (!entry) {
        fresh = true;
        entry = { role, index: i, email: `${environment}-${role}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`, password: randomBytes(24).toString("base64url"), id: randomUUID(), done: false };
        roster?.entries.push(entry); save(); // Persist identity before the first possibly ambiguous write.
      }
      const { email, password } = entry;
      let reply: Record<string, unknown>;
      if (roster && !fresh) {
        const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/login", {
          method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
        }, fetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
          if ((error as {code?:string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: login deadline expired; ${partial}`);
          remaining(); throw error;
        });
        reply = result.json;
        if (!reply.session && (entry.principal || (reply.denied as {code?:string})?.code !== "E_AUTH")) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: saved account login failed; inspect roster and deployment; no replacement account created");
      } else reply = {};
      if (!reply.session) {
      const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/signup", {
        method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
      }, fetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
        if ((error as {code?: string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: signup deadline expired; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
        remaining(); throw error;
      });
      reply = result.json;
      }
      if ((reply.denied as {code?:string} | undefined)?.code === "E_RATE_LIMIT") {
        throw Error("E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: identity retry budget exhausted; increase timeoutMs or inspect partial accounts/grants before retrying");
      }
      const session = reply.session as {principal?:string;kind?:string;token?:string;expiresAt?:number} | undefined;
      if (typeof session?.principal !== "string" || typeof session.token !== "string" || typeof session.expiresAt !== "number" || session.kind !== "user") {
        throw Error(`E_STAGING_SIGNUP: signup refused (${(reply.denied as {code?:string} | undefined)?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
      }
      const account: StagingAccount = { kind: session.kind, role, email, password, principal: session.principal, token: session.token, expiresAt: session.expiresAt };
      if (entry.principal && entry.principal !== account.principal) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: principal differs from saved roster; inspect deployment");
      entry.principal = account.principal; save();
      const args = { principal: account.principal, role, ...options.args?.(account) };
      const encodedArgs = canonical(args);
      if (entry.args && entry.args !== encodedArgs) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: provisioning arguments changed; inspect roster instead of silently reassigning roles");
      entry.args = encodedArgs; save();
      if (!entry.done) {
      const response = await request(`/m/${options.provision.name}`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: entry.id, args }) });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || result.state !== "sent") throw Error(`E_STAGING_PROVISION: ${options.provision.name} refused (${result.why?.code ?? result.denied?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); supplied arguments: ${Object.keys(args).sort().join(", ")}; default provision arguments are principal and role; use args(account) for custom names; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
      entry.done = true; save();
      }
      sessions[role]!.push(account);
    }
  }
  return sessions;
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
/** @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_STAGING for production/dev hosts; E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup. */
export function seedAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  return accounts(host, options, "staging");
}

function canonical(value: unknown): string {
  // Compare the JSON wire values, including nested objects, independent of key order.
  const sorted = (v: unknown): unknown => Array.isArray(v) ? v.map(sorted) : v && typeof v === "object"
    ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).sort(([a],[b]) => a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0).map(([k,x]) => [k,sorted(x)])) : v;
  return JSON.stringify(sorted(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value))));
}
interface RosterEntry { role: string; index: number; email: string; password: string; id: string; principal?: string; args?: string; done: boolean }
interface Roster { version: 1; plan: string; entries: RosterEntry[] }
/** Reuse a named deployment roster with fresh password sessions; provision only unfinished entries.
 * @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_PRODUCTION_ACCOUNTS for staging/dev;
 * E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER for changed/unsafe roster or failed saved login; E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER_BUSY for concurrent setup;
 * E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup.
 */
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#16-wave-27-process-checks-dx10 — persist credentials and stable IDs before production effects.
export async function provisionAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken"> & { deploymentDir?: string }, options: ProvisionAccountsOptions): Promise<ProvisionedSessions> {
  validatePlan(options);
  const name = options.roster ?? "default", directory = options.rosterDir ?? host.deploymentDir;
  if (!directory || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(name)) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: use startServe or supply rosterDir, and a simple roster name");
  const base = resolve(directory);
  if (!lstatSync(base).isDirectory()) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: rosterDir must be an existing deployment directory");
  const folder = join(base, "proof-accounts"); mkdirSync(folder, {mode:0o700, recursive:true});
  // Persist the newly created roster directory's link as well as each snapshot.
  const parent = openSync(base, "r"); try { fsyncSync(parent); } finally { closeSync(parent); }
  const path = join(folder, name + ".json"), lock = path + ".lock";
  let fd: number;
  try { fd = openSync(lock, "wx", 0o600); }
  catch { throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER_BUSY: roster already in use; after an interrupted driver inspect and remove its .lock before retrying"); }
  try {
    const plan = JSON.stringify({roles:Object.entries(options.roles).sort(), provision:options.provision.name, service:options.service ?? "operator"});
    let roster: Roster;
    try {
      if (!lstatSync(path).isFile() || (lstatSync(path).mode & 0o077)) throw Error("unsafe roster file");
      roster = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
      if (roster.version !== 1 || roster.plan !== plan || !Array.isArray(roster.entries)) throw Error("roster plan changed");
      const slots = new Set<string>();
      for (const entry of roster.entries) {
        if (!entry) throw Error("invalid roster entry");
        const slot = `${entry.role}:${entry.index}`;
        if (!entry || typeof entry.role !== "string" || !Number.isSafeInteger(entry.index) || entry.index < 0
            || entry.index >= (options.roles[entry.role] ?? 0) || slots.has(slot)
            || typeof entry.email !== "string" || !entry.email.endsWith("@example.test") || typeof entry.password !== "string" || entry.password.length < 20
            || typeof entry.id !== "string" || !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/.test(entry.id) || typeof entry.done !== "boolean"
            || (entry.principal !== undefined && typeof entry.principal !== "string") || (entry.args !== undefined && typeof entry.args !== "string")
            || (entry.done && (!entry.principal || !entry.args))) throw Error("invalid roster entry");
        slots.add(slot);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ENOENT") throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: unreadable, unsafe or changed roster; inspect it; no new accounts created");
      roster = {version:1, plan, entries:[]};
    }
    const save = () => {
      const temporary = path + "." + randomUUID() + ".tmp";
      const snapshot = openSync(temporary, "wx", 0o600);
      try { writeFileSync(snapshot, JSON.stringify(roster)); fsyncSync(snapshot); } finally { closeSync(snapshot); }
      renameSync(temporary, path);
      const parent = openSync(folder, "r"); try { fsyncSync(parent); } finally { closeSync(parent); }
    };
    return await accounts(host, options, "production", roster, save);
  } finally { closeSync(fd); unlinkSync(lock); }
}

/** Fresh password accounts with ordinary operator provisioning, for startDev acceptance proofs.
 * Same role/count/provision/args plan and account shape as production.
 * @throws invalid plan, non-development host, signup/provision refusal or bounded timeout. */
export function devAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  return accounts(host, options, "development");
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — a browser proof calls the app's own authenticated client.
import type { App } from "./app.js";
import type { Mutation, Write } from "./contract.js";

export type WitnessReason = "not-ready" | "returned-early" | "event" | "arguments";

type Operations = Record<string, unknown>;
export function registerWebWitness(app: App, api?: Operations): void {
  const handlerClaim = Symbol("witness explicit handler claim");
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.location?.search !== "string") return;
  const locked = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window, "__snapback4Witness");
  if (locked?.configurable === false) {
    (locked.value as { registerApi(owner: App, api?: Operations): void }).registerApi(app, api);
    return;
  }
  const enabled = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("witness") === "web";
  const armed = () => { if (!enabled) throw Object.assign(Error("E_WITNESS_DISABLED: add &witness=web"), { code: "E_WITNESS_DISABLED" }); };
  const operations: unknown[] = [];
  const walk = (value: Operations) => { for (const entry of Object.values(value)) {
    if (entry && typeof entry === "object") {
      if ("kind" in entry && entry.kind === "mutation" && !("ephemeral" in entry && entry.ephemeral === true)) operations.push(entry);
      else if (!("kind" in entry)) walk(entry as Operations);
    }
  } };
  if (api) walk(api);
  let ready = false;
  void app.ready.then(result => { ready = result.ok; }, () => {});
  let form: { terminal: Promise<unknown>; cancel: () => void; claimHandler: (owner: App, event: Event) => { mutate: App["mutate"]; done: () => void } | undefined } | undefined;
  Object.defineProperty(window, "__snapback4Witness", { configurable: true, value: {
    registerApi: (owner: App, operationsApi?: Operations) => {
      if (owner !== app) throw Error("E_WITNESS_APP_SELECTION: this proof selected another App; use a separate page for another application instance");
      if (operationsApi) { api = operationsApi; operations.length = 0; walk(operationsApi); }
    },
    ready: () => app.ready,
    viewer: () => app.client?.viewer(),
    clock: () => {
      armed();
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: no signed-in client clock");
      return app.client.now();
    },
    beginForm: (name: string | undefined, expected: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined, button: Element) => {
      armed();
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: no signed-in client");
      if (expected != null && (typeof expected !== "object" || Array.isArray(expected))) throw Error("E_WITNESS_ARGUMENTS: expected must be an argument constraint object or null for dynamic arguments");
      form?.cancel();
      const client = app.client, originalClient = client.mutate, originalApp = app.mutate;
      let click: Event | undefined, submit: Event | undefined, clickTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined, claimed = false, cancelObserver = () => {};
      let clickedReady = false, handlerClaimed = false;
      const handlerToken = Symbol("witness handler claim");
      const outsideClick = Symbol("witness outside clicked dispatch");
      let resolve!: (write: unknown) => void;
      const terminal = new Promise(answer => { resolve = answer; });
      let settled = false, noWriteTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      const noWrite = () => finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_NO_WRITE", reason: clickedReady ? "returned-early" : "not-ready", message: "armed click produced no durable mutation (form handler returned early, or the app was not ready)" } });
      const finish = (write: unknown) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true; clearTimeout(expiry); clearTimeout(noWriteTimer); restore(); cancelObserver(); resolve(write);
      };
      // Bound even an abandoned arm or a claimed write that never settles.
      const receiptExpired = () => finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_SENT", message: "claimed write did not settle within 30000 ms; inspect its original intent id" } });
      let expiry = setTimeout(noWrite, 30_000);
      // Chromium's active event ties admission to this actual control's handler.
      // Timers, input autosaves and previously-started writes cannot claim it.
      const capture = (event: Event) => {
        if (handlerClaimed) return; // A later click cannot restart the held handler timer.
        if (!event.isTrusted) { click = undefined; submit = undefined; clearTimeout(clickTimer); return; }
        if (event.isTrusted && event.composedPath().includes(button)) {
          click = event; submit = undefined; clickedReady = ready;
          clearTimeout(noWriteTimer);
          noWriteTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); }, 1000);
          clearTimeout(clickTimer);
          clickTimer = setTimeout(() => { click = undefined; submit = undefined; }, 0);
        }
      };
      const captureSubmit = (event: Event) => {
        if (click && event.isTrusted && (event as SubmitEvent).submitter === button) submit = event;
      };
      document.addEventListener("click", capture, true);
      document.addEventListener("submit", captureSubmit, true);
      const restore = () => {
        if (client.mutate === clientWrapper) client.mutate = originalClient;
        if (app.mutate === appWrapper) app.mutate = originalApp;
        document.removeEventListener("click", capture, true);
        document.removeEventListener("submit", captureSubmit, true); clearTimeout(clickTimer);
      };
      const canonical = (value: unknown): unknown => Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(canonical) : value && typeof value === "object"
        ? Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(value).sort().map(key => [key, canonical((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key])])) : value;
      const execute = async (original: Function, receiver: unknown, op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any): Promise<Write> => {
        const durable = api ? operations.some(value => (value as Mutation<any, any>).name === op.name) : op.kind === "mutation" && (op as { ephemeral?: boolean }).ephemeral !== true;
        const eventMatches = options?.[handlerClaim] !== undefined
          ? handlerClaimed && options[handlerClaim] === handlerToken
          : !!click && (window.event === click || (!!submit && window.event === submit));
        // An explicit object constrains its named arguments. Generated IDs and
        // other handler-owned arguments remain in the real claimed write.
        const argsMatch = expected == null || Object.keys(expected).every(key => Object.hasOwn(args, key) && JSON.stringify(canonical(expected[key])) === JSON.stringify(canonical(args[key])));
        const eligible = !claimed && eventMatches && !options?.[outsideClick] && durable && (!name || name === op.name) && argsMatch;
        let forwarded = options;
        if (!eligible && receiver === app && durable) {
          const { id, waitSeconds, ...other } = options ?? {};
          forwarded = { ...other, id, waitSeconds, [outsideClick]: true };
        }
        const invoke = () => original.call(receiver, op, args, forwarded) as Promise<Write>;
        if (!eligible) {
          if (!claimed && durable) {
            claimed = true; restore();
            const reason = !eventMatches || options?.[outsideClick] ? "event: expected the armed click or its synchronous submit"
              : name && name !== op.name ? `operation: expected ${name}, got ${op.name}`
              : `arguments: mismatched keys: ${[...new Set([...Object.keys(expected ?? {}), ...Object.keys(args)])].sort().filter(key => JSON.stringify(canonical(expected?.[key])) !== JSON.stringify(canonical(args[key]))).join(", ")}`;
            if (eventMatches && !options?.[outsideClick] && (!name || name === op.name) && !argsMatch) {
              const diagnostic = (window as unknown as { __snapback4ProofDiagnostic?: (value: unknown) => Promise<void> }).__snapback4ProofDiagnostic;
              // The proof driver writes values only to its mode-0600 private log.
              // A failed diagnostic must never prevent the application's write.
              try { void diagnostic?.({ code: "E_WITNESS_UNCLAIMED", mutation: op.name, armed: expected, actual: args }).catch(() => {}); } catch { /* Diagnostic failure cannot block the write. */ }
            }
            finish({ state: "failed", mutation: op.name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_UNCLAIMED", reason: !eventMatches || options?.[outsideClick] ? "event" : "arguments", message: reason } });
          }

        }
        claimed = true; restore(); clearTimeout(expiry); clearTimeout(noWriteTimer);
        // A late click receives its own complete settlement allowance.
        expiry = setTimeout(receiptExpired, 30_000);
        let id: string | undefined, early: Write[] = [];
        const stop = client.onWrite(write => {
          // Native clients publish terminal receipts; foreign adapters must not
          // turn a pending notification into terminal proof evidence either.
          if ((write as Write).state === "pending") return;
          if (id === undefined) { early.push(write); if (early.length > 256) early.shift(); }
          else if (write.id === id) { stop(); finish({ ...write, mutation: op.name }); }
        });
        cancelObserver = stop;
        try {
          const write = await invoke();
          id = write.id;
          const receipt = write.state === "pending" ? early.find(value => value.id === id && value.state !== "pending") : write;
          early = [];
          if (receipt) { stop(); finish({ ...receipt, mutation: op.name }); }

        } catch (error) { stop(); finish({ state: "failed", why: { code: "E_WITNESS_WEB" } }); throw error; }
      };

      const clientWrapper = ((op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any) => execute(originalClient, client, op, args, options)) as typeof client.mutate;
      const appWrapper = ((op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any) => execute(originalApp, app, op, args, options)) as typeof app.mutate;
      client.mutate = clientWrapper; app.mutate = appWrapper;

      const claimHandler = (owner: App, event: Event) => {
        if (owner !== app || handlerClaimed || claimed || settled || !event.isTrusted
          || !(click && event === click && window.event === click || submit && event === submit && window.event === submit)) return undefined;
        handlerClaimed = true; clearTimeout(noWriteTimer);
        return {
          mutate: ((op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any) =>
            app.mutate(op, args, { ...options, [handlerClaim]: handlerToken })) as App["mutate"],
          done: () => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); },
        };
      };
      form = { terminal, claimHandler, cancel: () => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); else finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_UNCLAIMED", reason: "event", message: "rearmed before the claimed write settled; inspect its original intent id" } }); } };
    },
    claimHandler: (owner: App, event: Event) => form?.claimHandler(owner, event),
    formResult: () => form?.terminal,
    mutate: async (name: string | undefined, args: Record<string, unknown>, id: string) => {
      armed();
      const operation = operations.find(value => !name || (value as { name: string }).name === name);
      if (!operation) throw Error("E_WITNESS_MUTATION: no matching durable mutation; pass --mutation with a generated API name (ephemeral signals do not prove stored writes)");
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: the app has no signed-in client");
      let observed: unknown;
      let settle: ((write: unknown) => void) | undefined;
      const stop = app.client.onWrite(write => { if (write.id === id) { observed = write; settle?.(write); } });
      try {
        const write = await app.mutate(operation as Mutation<any, any>, args, { id, waitSeconds: 0 });
        if (write.state !== "pending") return write;
        return observed ?? await new Promise(resolve => { settle = resolve; });
      } finally { stop(); }
    },
  } });
}

/** @internal Shared by the Node proof kit and the native browser driver. */
export function webWitnessObserverScript(): string {
  return `(() => {
    const register = (${registerWebWitness.toString()});
    let selected;
    Object.defineProperty(window, Symbol.for("snapback4.proof.app"), { configurable: false, writable: false, value: app => {
      if (selected) return;
      selected = app;
      register(app);
      const bridge = window.__snapback4Witness;
      Object.freeze(bridge);
      Object.defineProperty(window, "__snapback4Witness", { configurable: false, writable: false, value: bridge });
    }});
  })();`;
}

pub fn rollback(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "rollback",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let (_deployment, held) = activation_target(&dir)?;
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let active = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            }
        "rollback",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let (_deployment, held) = activation_target(&dir)?;
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let active = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            }
            .ok_or("no current release")?;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            let selected = options
                .flags
                .get("to")
                .map(|n| {
                    if n=="latest" {return latest(&dir).map_err(|_| "no complete latest release");}
                    n.parse::<u64>()
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|n| *n > 0)
                        .ok_or("--to requires a positive immutable release id")
                })
                .transpose()?;
            let previous = selected
                .or_else(|| held["previous_active"].as_u64())
                .ok_or("nothing to roll back to")?;
            let logical=super::rollback_views::metadata(&dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()))?.map(|v|v.target).unwrap_or(previous);
            let mut view=if options.flags.contains_key("exact"){None}else{super::rollback_views::resolve(&dir,logical)?};
            if let Some(registered)=&view {
                if super::rollback_views::prefer_original(&dir,registered,options,_deployment.as_ref())? {view=None;}
            }
            let previous=view.as_ref().map(|v|v.release).unwrap_or(logical);
            if let Some(view)=&view {
                if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {eprintln!("rollback target: release {} via bridge {} (registered by release {})",view.target,view.release,view.parent);
                for reason in &view.hides {eprintln!("intentional rollback hiding: {reason}");}}
            }
            let mut selected_options=options.clone();
            selected_options.flags.insert("output-verb".into(),"rollback".into());
            if !options.flags.contains_key("exact"){selected_options.flags.insert("visible-rollback".into(),String::new());}
            if let Some(view)=&view{selected_options.flags.insert("rollback-view".into(),serde_json::to_string(view).unwrap());}
            let options=&selected_options;
            selectable(&dir, previous)?;
            let bound = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()))?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#14-wave-14-rollback-narration — resolve bare/latest selection visibly before any preview or live activation.
            let target_line=format!("resolved rollback target: release {previous} (current release {active})");
            if options.json {eprintln!("{target_line}");} else {println!("{target_line}");}
            let legacy_refusal=|error:String| {
                if view.is_none() && !options.flags.contains_key("exact") && parent_predates_views(&dir.join("releases").join(active.to_string())) {
                    format!("{error}; this parent predates rollback views; deploy the current source with this CLI to register needed views, or explicitly select the historical workflow with `snapback4 rollback {} --to {logical} --exact` (preview its hiding with --check first)",dir.display())
                }else{error}
            };
            let mut bridge_options=options.clone();
            if let Some(path)=bridge_options.flags.remove("bridge") {
                super::release_bridge::write(Path::new(&path),&dir,active,previous,&[])?;
                eprintln!("bridge starter written to {path}; follow ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md before exact rollback");
            }
            let options=&bridge_options;
            check_change(&dir, Some(active), &bound, options, Some(previous)).map_err(&legacy_refusal)?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                return super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()),previous,options,_deployment.as_ref()).map_err(&legacy_refusal);
            }
            activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                previous,
                Some(active),
                revision,
                bound,
                true,
                true,
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
                None,
            ).map_err(&legacy_refusal)?;

            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn releases(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "releases",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let after = options
                .flags
                .get("after")
                .map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| "--after requires an event id"))
                .transpose()?
                .unwrap_or(0);
            let event = options
                .flags
                .get("event")
                .map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| "--event requires an event id"))
                .transpose()?;
            // Read-only inspection needs no ownership handoff or source adoption.
            let mut store = snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let retained = store.dormant_inventory(None, usize::MAX).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let mut result = store
                .release_status(
                    after,
                    options.flags.get("items-after").map(String::as_str),
                    event,
                )
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if result["release"].is_null() { result["release"]=serde_json::json!(inspect_current(&dir)?); }
            result["current"]=result["release"].clone();
            result["live"]=result["release"].as_u64().map(|n|live_identity(&dir,n)).transpose()?.unwrap_or(serde_json::Value::Null);
            result["latest"]=serde_json::json!(latest(&dir).ok());
            let mut views=Vec::new();for parent in ordinary_releases(&dir)?{views.extend(super::rollback_views::manifest(&dir.join("releases").join(parent.to_string()))?);}
            result["rollbackViews"]=serde_json::to_value(views).unwrap();
            if let Some(selector)=options.positionals.get(1) {
                let n=if selector=="latest" {latest(&dir)?} else if selector=="current" {result["release"].as_u64().ok_or("no current release")?} else {selector.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or("releases selection requires N, latest or current")?};
                selectable(&dir,n)?;let root=dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string());bound_at(root.clone())?;
                result["selected"]=serde_json::json!({"release":n,"current":result["release"].as_u64()==Some(n),"digest":crate::release_files::digest(&root)?});
            }
            if options.json||options.flags.contains_key("verbose")||options.flags.contains_key("review") {println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap());}
            else {
                println!("releases verdict: READY; current {}; latest {}; generation {}",result["current"],result["latest"],result["generation"]);
                print_live(&result["live"]);
                println!("{} events; {} dormant items on this page; next: releases --json for full evidence and paging; --review for details",result["events"].as_array().map_or(0,Vec::len),result["items"].as_array().map_or(0,Vec::len));
                if let Some(selected)=result.get("selected") {println!("selected: {selected}");}
            }
            super::sql::dormant_notice(super::sql::dormant_rows(&retained), "use `snapback4 sql 'SELECT …' --release <dir> --dormant` to inspect them");
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn prune(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "prune",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let owner = target(&dir)?;
            let held = status(&dir, owner.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            let confirm = options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive");
            let result = match owner.as_ref() {
                Some(owner) => owner
                    .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
                    .and_then(|mut s| s.prune_dormant(confirm, Some(revision)))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
                None => crate::release_control::request(
                    &dir,
                    crate::release_control::Command::Prune {
                        confirm,
                        expected_revision: revision,
                    },
                )?,
            };
            println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap());

//! DX12 changes presentation only; full evidence and refusal status survive.
use std::{fs,path::PathBuf,process::{Command,Output}};
struct App(PathBuf);
impl App {
 fn new()->Self {let p=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dx12-output-{}-{}",std::process::id(),{static N:std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize=std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0);N.fetch_add(1,std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)}));fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();Self(p)}
 fn write(&self,text:&str){fs::write(self.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),text).unwrap();}
 fn run(&self,args:&[&str])->Output{Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4")).args(args).current_dir(&self.0).output().unwrap()}
 fn ok(&self,args:&[&str])->String{let r=self.run(args);assert!(r.status.success(),"{args:?}: {r:?}");String::from_utf8(r.stdout).unwrap()}
}
impl Drop for App{fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
const SOURCE:&str="table notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  public 'fixture'\nquery list():\n  return notes first 20 by .id\n";
#[test]fn deploy_rollback_and_releases_lead_with_verdict_and_keep_live_identity(){
 let app=App::new();app.write(SOURCE);
 let first=app.ok(&["deploy","production"]);assert!(first.starts_with("deploy verdict: READY"),"{first}");assert!(first.lines().nth(1).unwrap().starts_with("live:"),"{first}");assert!(!first.contains("decision "),"{first}");
 app.write(&SOURCE.replace("<=80","<=160"));let second=app.ok(&["deploy","production"]);assert!(!second.contains("preview:"),"{second}");
 let rolled=app.ok(&["rollback","production","--to","1"]);assert!(rolled.starts_with("rollback verdict: READY"),"{rolled}");assert!(rolled.lines().nth(1).unwrap().starts_with("live:"),"{rolled}");
 let short=app.ok(&["releases","production"]);assert!(short.starts_with("releases verdict: READY"),"{short}");assert!(short.lines().count()<=4,"{short}");
 let json:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_str(&app.ok(&["releases","production","--json"])).unwrap();assert!(json["live"].is_object());assert!(json["events"].is_array());
 let review=app.ok(&["deploy","other","--review"]);assert!(review.contains("decision ")||review.contains("REVIEW"),"{review}");
}
#[test]fn gates_remain_explicit_and_blocking_with_next_steps(){
 let app=App::new();app.write("table docs:\n  photo: image <=10000 ?\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- deny\n  online only\ninvariant docs always .photo != null\nmutation add(photo: image <=10000 ?):\n  return insert docs {photo}\n");
 let r=app.run(&["deploy","production"]);assert!(!r.status.success());let err=String::from_utf8_lossy(&r.stderr);assert!(err.contains("deploy verdict: REFUSED")&&err.contains("N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE")&&err.contains("Intended:")&&err.contains("Repair:"),"{err}");
 let json:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_slice(&app.run(&["deploy","production","--json"]).stdout).unwrap();assert_eq!(json["ok"],false);assert!(json["security"]["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|d|d["gated"]==true&&d["acknowledged"]==false));
}
    let rule_enforced=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&v.direct_rule==security::Status::Proved).count();
    eprintln!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted; {rule_enforced} enforced by rule (additional paths not proved); {} other unproved ({} failing)",report.invariants.len()-proved-refuted-rule_enforced,failures.len());
    for v in &failures {
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        eprintln!("  FIX {}: {} via {} as {} ({}); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run snapback4 test",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,serde_json::to_value(&c.reachability).unwrap().as_str().unwrap());
    }
    let supplemental=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|!c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    if supplemental>0 {eprintln!("  {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing)");}
    if verbose {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);eprint!("{}",human.text_with_all(true));}
    else if !report.invariants.is_empty()||!report.campaign_coverage.is_empty(){eprintln!("  next: snapback4 test; {} coverage gaps (check --verbose/JSON keeps every reason)",report.campaign_coverage.len());}
    if !report.exposures.is_empty(){eprintln!("  review audiences and {} uncovered exposures: snapback4 audit",report.uncovered.len());}
}

/// A rule-changing preflight demands a current campaign, without pretending
/// finite tests or undeclared intent certify a deployment.
pub(super) fn release_loop(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],prior:Option<&Bound>,options:&Options)->Result<Option<String>,String>{
    let changed=match prior {
        None=>true,
        Some(old)=>serde_json::to_vec(&old.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?,
    };
    if !changed{return Ok(None);}
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#153-rule-changing-deployment-review — review is prominent, strictness is explicit and failure-origin aware.
    let started=std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut preflight=options.clone();if !options.flags.contains_key("staging")||options.flags.contains_key("strict"){preflight.flags.insert("security-deploy".into(),String::new());}
    let mut report=report(project,bound,sources,&preflight);
    report.notes.extend(presence::notes(project,bound,prior,sources)?);
    if super::proof_source::sources(project,options,"schema")?!=sources{return Err("selected Quarry sources changed during deployment security preflight".into());}
    let dispositions=super::security_reviews::review_snapshot(project,sources,&report.notes)?;
    let derived=security::derived::notes(sources,bound);
    let acknowledged=derived.iter().filter(|n|report.notes.iter().zip(&dispositions).any(|(r,d)|r.site==n.site&&r.code==n.code&&r.message==n.message&&d.acknowledged)).map(|n|n.site.to_string()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
    let pending=super::security_reviews::pending(&dispositions);
    let fixes=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())&&(!security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant)||!acknowledged.contains(&v.invariant.site.to_string()))).count();
    if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") { eprintln!("security deploy campaign: {} ms; bounded replay, remaining paths retain coverage gaps",started.elapsed().as_millis()); }
    let scope=release_scope(bound,prior,sources);
    let review=options.flags.contains_key("review")||options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
    let mut notice=String::new();
    for v in report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())){
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        if security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant)&&acknowledged.contains(&v.invariant.site.to_string()){
            if review {notice.push_str(&format!("  REVIEWED derived {}: individual specification decision retained\n",v.invariant.target_label()));} continue;
        }
        notice.push_str(&format!("  FIX {} {}: refuted via {} as {} ({:?}); repair enforcement to match intent, then run snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,c.reachability));
        if !security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant) {
            if v.invariant.target=="write"&&v.invariant.column.is_some()&&c.effect=="insert" {
                notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if broader creation is deliberate, replace this assertion with `invariant {}.{} update only {}`; then run snapback4 test. Authored contradictions cannot be cleared with --ack-security\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table,v.invariant.column.as_ref().unwrap(),v.invariant.predicate));
            }else {
                notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if this observed audience is deliberate, revise the authored assertion here to match that specification, then run snapback4 test; --ack-security applies only to derived questions, never an authored contradiction\n",v.invariant.site));
            }
        }
        let aggregates=bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.source==v.invariant.table).map(|m|m.target.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if c.observations.is_some()&&!aggregates.is_empty()&&v.invariant.target=="read"&&v.invariant.column.is_none() {
            notice.push_str(&format!("  DERIVED {}: a separately readable aggregate changes in the paired observation; tightening the source read rule alone cannot clear this disclosure\n",aggregates.join(", ")));
            let field=v.invariant.predicate.split('.').skip(1).filter_map(|part|part.split(|c:char|!c.is_alphanumeric()&&c!='_').next()).find(|field|bound.schema.table(&v.invariant.table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(*field)).is_some_and(|kind|matches!(kind.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)));
            if let Some(field)=field {notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if aggregate counts are intentionally public, replace the whole-row assertion with `invariant {}.{} read only {}` for this protected field and independently declare any other private fields; otherwise restrict the aggregate audience; rerun snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table,field,v.invariant.predicate));}
        }else if v.invariant.target=="read"&&v.invariant.column.is_none() {
            for (file,text) in sources {
                let doc=snapback4_lang::parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
                if let Some(table)=doc.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==v.invariant.table) {
                    if let Some(rule)=table.rules.iter().find(|r|r.effect==snapback4_lang::ast::Effect::Read) {
                        let expanded=bound.refusal_sites.get(&(v.invariant.table.clone(),"$read-rule".into()));
                        if expanded.is_some() {
                            notice.push_str(&format!("  SOURCE {}: preserve `{}` and its parent lifetime/authority; if this audience is unintended, repair the parent read rule before rerunning snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.table,snapback4_lang::bind::spell_internals(&snapback4_lang::print::expression(&rule.value))));
                        }else {
                            notice.push_str(&format!("  SOURCE {}: retain the current audience and enforce this declared intent with `read <- ({}) and ({})`; rerun snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.table,snapback4_lang::print::expression(&rule.value),v.invariant.predicate));
                        }
                        let predicate=expanded.cloned().unwrap_or_else(||snapback4_lang::print::expression(&rule.value));
                        notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if every reader admitted by the current read rule is deliberately allowed, replace this assertion with `invariant {}.read only {predicate}`; then run snapback4 test and deploy again. This is an authored specification change, not --ack-security\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table));
                    }
                }
            }
        }

    }
    let mut notes=report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code!="N_SECURITY_INTENT"&&scope.iter().any(|(file,start,end)|n.site.file==*file&&n.site.line>=*start&&n.site.line<*end)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    notes.sort_by_key(|n|crate::note_rank::summary_order(&n.code));
    let count=notes.len();
    // Never suppress a gate or write-authority question behind the display cap.
    for (i,n) in notes.into_iter().enumerate(){if review&&crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review&&(i<5||security::note_priority(&n.code)<=3){notice.push_str(&format!("  REVIEW {} {}: {}\n    {}\n",n.site,n.code,n.message,n.rewrite));}}
    if review&&count>5 {notice.push_str(&format!("  {count} changed-scope notes in deploy --check --json\n"));}
    let selected=report.notes.iter().zip(&dispositions).filter(|(n,d)|d.gated || n.code!="N_SECURITY_INTENT" && scope.iter().any(|(file,start,end)| n.site.file==*file&&n.site.line>=*start&&n.site.line<*end)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let complete_family=dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.family.is_some()).count()==selected.iter().filter(|(_,d)|d.family.is_some()).count();
    let scoped_notes=selected.iter().map(|(n,_)|(*n).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let scoped_dispositions=selected.iter().map(|(_,d)| {let mut d=(*d).clone(); if !complete_family {d.family=None;} d}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // A scoped deploy cannot silently acknowledge unseen family members. It
    // prints their individual commands; ordinary check offers the whole family.
    notice.push_str(&super::security_reviews::render_mode(&scoped_dispositions,&scoped_notes,review));
    let test=release_notice(project,bound,sources,options);
    let advice=scoped_dispositions.iter().filter(|d|!d.gated).count();
    notice.push_str(&format!("{}; {advice} advisory decisions: deploy --review (or check --review)\n",enforced_text(&report,bound).replacen("check:","security:",1)));
    if review||test.contains("run `snapback4 test") {notice.push_str(&format!("  {test}\n"));}
    let verdict=format!("security FIX {fixes}; {} pending review",dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged).count());
    Ok(Some(serde_json::json!({"notice":notice,"verdict":verdict,"fixes":fixes,"pending":pending,"changedNotes":count,"report":report,"securityReviews":dispositions}).to_string()))
}
fn age(timestamp:Option<i64>,now:i64)->String{
    timestamp.map(|t|format!("at {t} ms since Unix epoch, age {}s",now.saturating_sub(t).max(0)/1000)).unwrap_or_else(||"time/age unknown (legacy record)".into())
}
fn release_notice(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],options:&Options)->String{
    let selection=options.flags.get("schema").map(|s|format!(" --source {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let fixture=options.flags.get("fixture").map(|s|format!(" --fixture {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let now=snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let matching=record::matching(project,options,sources);
    let matches=matching.is_some();
    match matching.or_else(||record::latest(project)){
        Some(record)=>{
            let result=if !record.complete{"incomplete"}else if record.run_violations.unwrap_or(record.violations.len())>0{"FAILED"}else{"completed, no observed violation"};
            let current=if matches{"matches selected source/fixtures/CLI"}else{"STALE: source, fixtures or CLI differ; does not qualify selected release"};
            let next=if matches&&record.complete{"matching test campaign recorded (finite, not proof)".into()}else{format!("run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment")};
            format!("last test: {result}, {}; {current}; {} retained reachable refutations, {} coverage gaps; last completed test {}; {next}",age(record.recorded_at,now),record.violations.len(),record.coverage_gaps,age(record.last_completed_at,now))
        },
        None=>format!("last test: NEVER RECORDED; run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment; no matching completed campaign for this source, fixtures and CLI; {} independent invariants",bound.invariants.len()),
    }
}

// SEC7 owns ordinary check ranking/gates; this scope and output belong to ROLL.
//! Individually justified source-bound dispositions; never proof or authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — bulk compiler-note review cannot clear security gates.
use super::*;
use serde::{Serialize,Deserialize};
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
const ANALYZER:&str="sec11-1";
mod binding;
mod decisions;
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String,#[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")] witness:Option<serde_json::Value>}
#[derive(Clone,Serialize)]
pub(super) struct Disposition{pub family:Option<String>,pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub recorded:bool,pub reason:Option<String>,pub scope:String,pub witness:Option<serde_json::Value>}
pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{snapback4_lang::security::gated_note(code)}
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String{format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,ack,false,&[])
}
/// Read-only decisions for a caller-validated immutable saved-source selection.
pub(super) fn review_snapshot(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,None,true,&[])
}
pub(super) fn review_evidence(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>,evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,ack,false,evidence)
}
fn review_inner(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>,snapshot:bool,evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    if !snapshot&&project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    let identities=binding::identities(notes,sources);
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    // Keep the lock inode in ignored generated output. Do not unlink a lock
    // while another process can be waiting on that inode. Kernel ownership
    // releases it on exit, including an interrupted writer.
    let _writer_lock=if ack.is_some(){
        let directory=project.schema_dir().join("generated");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|e|format!("cannot create security review lock directory: {e}"))?;
        let lock=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).truncate(false).read(true).write(true).open(directory.join("security-reviews.lock")).map_err(|e|format!("cannot open security review lock: {e}"))?;
        lock.lock().map_err(|e|format!("cannot acquire security review lock: {e}"))?;Some(lock)
    }else{None};
    if !snapshot&&project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed while waiting for security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut file:File=match std::fs::read(&path){
        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    };
    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    let mut recorded=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
        // Validate the complete batch against the same captured source/note set.
        // Nothing is published when even one member is stale or unknown.
        let mut selected=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        for key in key.split(',').map(str::trim) {
            if key.starts_with("audience:") {
                let Some(family)=decisions::family(notes,&identities) else {return Err("no current advisory audience family; run check --review --json".into());};
                if family==key {
                    selected.extend(notes.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_,note)|decisions::audience(&note.code)).map(|(i,_)|i));
                } else {
                    let history: BTreeMap<String,Vec<(String,String)>>=std::fs::read(project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-families.json")).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
                    let members=history.get(key).ok_or("stale or unknown audience family: rerun check --review; existing individual decisions are preserved")?;
                    for (id,fingerprint) in members {
                        let index=identities.iter().enumerate().find(|(i,(current,binding,_))|current==id&&binding==fingerprint&&decisions::audience(&notes[*i].code)).map(|(i,_)|i).ok_or("stale audience family member: a relevant rule changed or disappeared; no decisions recorded; run check --review")?;
                        selected.insert(index);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                let index=identities.iter().position(|(id,_,_)|id==key).ok_or_else(||format!("--ack-security {key} is stale, belongs to another source selection, or is not a decision ID. Use securityReviews[].id from `check --review --json` (not a diagnostic code or invariant site); repeated --ack-security ID or comma-separated IDs share one --reason"))?;
                selected.insert(index);
            }
        }
        recorded=selected.clone();
        for index in selected {
            let (id,fingerprint,_)=&identities[index];
            file.entries.insert(id.clone(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into(),witness:evidence.get(index).cloned().flatten()});
        }
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|{
            if project.sources()?!=sources{return Err(std::io::Error::other("Quarry sources changed before disposition publication; rerun check"));}
            std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path)
        });
        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    }
    let family=decisions::family(notes,&identities);
    if !snapshot && let Some(family)=&family {
        let path=project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-families.json");
        let mut history:BTreeMap<String,Vec<(String,String)>>=std::fs::read(&path).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
        history.insert(family.clone(),identities.iter().zip(notes).filter(|(_,n)|decisions::audience(&n.code)).map(|((id,binding,_),_)|(id.clone(),binding.clone())).collect());
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let _=std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap());
        if std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec(&history).unwrap()).is_ok(){let _=std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path);}let _=std::fs::remove_file(temporary);
    }
    Ok(notes.iter().zip(identities).enumerate().map(|(i,(n,(key,fingerprint,scope)))|{let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|e.sources==fingerprint&&e.analyzer==ANALYZER);
        Disposition{family:decisions::audience(&n.code).then(||family.clone()).flatten(),id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:gated(&n.code),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),recorded:recorded.contains(&i),scope,reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone()),witness:entry.and_then(|e|e.witness.clone()).or_else(||evidence.get(i).cloned().flatten())}
    }).collect())
}
pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
pub(super) use decisions::{print,render,render_mode,count as decision_count};

 let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec7-dispositions-{}",std::process::id()));std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();
 let _scratch=Scratch(root.clone());let p=Project{root:root.clone()};std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return true\n").unwrap();let sources=p.sources().unwrap();
 let a=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","a","");let b=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT","b","");let notes=[a.clone(),b];let key=binding::identities(&notes,&sources)[0].0.clone();
 assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&key,"  "))).is_err());assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some(("unknown","intended"))).is_err());
 let ds=review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&key,"the specification allows optional draft evidence"))).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);assert!(pending(&ds));
 let changed=[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","changed","")];assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&changed,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 let path=p.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");let mut f:File=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(&path).unwrap()).unwrap();for old in ["sec7-2","sec7-3","sec7-4","sec7-5","sec7-6","sec7-7","sec7-8","sec7-9","sec9-1","sec9-3"]{f.entries.get_mut(&key).unwrap().analyzer=old.into();std::fs::write(&path,serde_json::to_vec(&f).unwrap()).unwrap();assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);}
 std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return false\n").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&key,"intended"))).is_err());assert!(!review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 std::fs::write(&path,"{}").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).is_err());std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
}
#[test]fn repeated_advisories_are_individually_reviewed_and_survive_comments(){
 let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec8-advisory-{}",std::process::id()));std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();let _scratch=Scratch(root.clone());let p=Project{root};
 let text="query q():\n  return true\n";std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),text).unwrap();
 let mut n=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&snapback4_lang::Site::new("schema.q",2,3),"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","q.read: reviewed audience","confirm spec");let notes=vec![n.clone(),n.clone()];
 let ds=review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).unwrap();assert_ne!(ds[0].id,ds[1].id);
 let ds=review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,Some((&ds[0].id,"Specification allows this audience"))).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);assert!(!pending(&ds));
 std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),format!("-- comment\n{text}")).unwrap();n.site.line+=1;
 let ds=review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&[n.clone(),n],None).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);
}

}

#[cfg(test)] mod family_tests {
 use super::*;
 #[test] fn audience_command_records_current_members_but_never_gates_or_changed_families() {
  let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-dx9-family-{}",std::process::id()));
  std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();let _scratch=super::tests::Scratch(root.clone());
  let p=Project{root};std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return true\n").unwrap();let sources=p.sources().unwrap();
  let n=|code:&str,message:&str|snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),code,message,"confirm");
  let notes=vec![n("N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","notes.read: audience A"),n("N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE","notes.write: audience B"),n("N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING","gate")];
  let ds=review(&p,&sources,&notes,None).unwrap();let family=ds[0].family.clone().unwrap();assert_eq!(ds[1].family.as_ref(),Some(&family));assert!(ds[2].family.is_none());
  let recorded=review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&family,"The specification admits both audiences"))).unwrap();
  assert!(recorded[0].acknowledged&&recorded[1].acknowledged);assert!(!recorded[2].acknowledged);assert!(pending(&recorded));
  let mut changed=notes.clone();changed.push(n("N_BROAD_READ","extra.read: another audience"));
  let retained=review(&p,&sources,&changed,Some((&family,"old decision"))).unwrap();
  assert!(retained[0].acknowledged&&retained[1].acknowledged);assert!(!retained[2].acknowledged&&!retained[3].acknowledged);
  assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&family,"  "))).is_err());
 }
}
